- **mass-spectra-fingerprint-tokenization**: Use when preparing experimental MS/MS spectra (e.g. MassSpecGym or NPLIB1) as token inputs for a fingerprint-to-structure generative model, and spectra must be converted into a condition-invariant discrete representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-fingerprint-tokenization))
- **mass-spectra-inference-latency-benchmarking**: Use when comparing end-to-end inference cost of a mass-spectra-to-structure generative model against diffusion/iterative baselines, or when an acceptance criterion specifies a per-spectrum latency and speed-up factor to reproduce. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-inference-latency-benchmarking))
- **mass-spectra-manifest-curation**: Use when preparing held-out EI-MS test sets from spectral libraries and you must prevent train/test compound overlap, remove deuterium-labeled compounds, or enforce the inference model's input limits (peak count, m/z range, SMILES length). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-manifest-curation))
- **mass-spectra-metadata-cleaning**: Use when importing a GNPS or similar MS/MS spectral library whose metadata and peaks must be cleaned, corrected, and filtered (e.g., ALL_GNPS.json) before building training/query subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-metadata-cleaning))
- **mass-spectra-metadata-harmonization**: Use when concatenating OMSLs from GNPS (.MGF), MoNA (.MSP/.SDF/.JSON), MassBank, or MS-Dial-VS17 into a single library and you need to harmonize metadata fields (SMILES, InChI, precursor m/z, adduct, ion mode, instrument type) so the output is usable with MS-Dial, MZmine, and Flash Entropy Search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-metadata-harmonization))
- **mass-spectra-peak-centroiding**: Use when raw profile-mode spectra or transients from MALDI FT-ICR MS (or reconstructed high-resolution equivalents) must be reduced to peak lists before m/z binning, TIC normalization, feature counting, or SNR comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-peak-centroiding))
- **mass-spectra-peak-filtering**: Use when raw GNPS MS/MS library spectra in MGF/mzML format must be cleaned and intensity-normalized before spectral similarity model training or inference, and peak count/intensity distributions are noisy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-peak-filtering--7b28be9064fa4584))
- **mass-spectra-peak-filtering**: Use when raw MGF MS/MS spectra must be cleaned before clustering or spectral-library building and peak information dominates runtime (&gt;95% of raw-file data volume). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-peak-filtering--1c9d286c0809ffbe))
- **mass-spectra-peak-matching**: Use when comparing a query tandem mass spectrum against reference spectra (e.g., ESI-MS2 peak lists of authentic compounds) to produce a similarity score for compound identification, as in MassBank's Spectral Search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-peak-matching))
- **mass-spectra-peak-parsing**: Use when a mass spectrum's peaks must be converted from a tabular or database record form (e.g. a MassBank record like PR100026) into the single-line 'ion:intensity' string format that SPLASH tools expect. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-peak-parsing))
- **mass-spectra-peak-tokenization**: Use when an in-silico EI-MS library of predicted spectra (e.g. NEIMS predictions over f-NIST and f-ChEMBL) must be transformed into fixed-length spectral embeddings to enable approximate nearest-neighbor matching with HNSW. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-peak-tokenization))
- **mass-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when raw profile MS2 lipid spectra must be converted into clean peak lists for downstream model training or spectral-library building, particularly when noisy spectra should be filtered by a base-peak-normalized median intensity threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectra-preprocessing-and-binning**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra (mgf, msp, mzML, mzXML, json, usi) must be converted into normalized, binned vectors before training MS2DeepScore or computing spectral embeddings; triggered by GNPS-style peak lists with heterogeneous intensity scales. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-preprocessing-and-binning))
- **mass-spectra-preprocessing-and-bucketing**: Use when raw MS/MS MGF files must be cleaned (peak filtering, intensity normalization, short/low-peak spectra dropped) and partitioned into precursor-m/z buckets prior to pairwise-distance clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-preprocessing-and-bucketing))
- **mass-spectra-reduced-spectrum-extraction**: Use when a peak list (ion:intensity pairs) must be converted into the SPLASH second summary block, or when a 4-digit base-36 histogram block must be reproduced or verified against a known SPLASH identifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-reduced-spectrum-extraction))
- **mass-spectra-similarity-network-construction**: Use when thousands-to-millions of tandem MS/MS spectra from LC-MS/MS datasets (e.g. GNPS MSV datasets) must be grouped by fragmentation-pattern similarity, either to propagate peptide/spectrum identifications across related spectra or to estimate true chemical diversity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mass-spectra-similarity-network-construction))
- **mass-spectra-to-smiles-translation**: Use when you have a preprocessed low-resolution GC-EI-MS spectrum (intensity-binned, threshold-filtered) and need de novo molecular structure candidates as SMILES strings without a reference spectral library match. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectra-to-smiles-translation))
- **mass-spectra-tokenization-unified-vocabulary**: Use when when you have paired mass spectra and molecular structure data and need to train a unified model for structure elucidation. Use this skill at the data preparation stage before pretraining, when you want both modalities to share representational capacity rather than operate in isolation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectra-tokenization-unified-vocabulary) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-adduct-repair**: Use when annotated MS/MS spectra (e.g. from GNPS or MoNA libraries) carry an adduct or parent mass inconsistent with their SMILES annotation and precursor m/z, and the annotation needs repair before library matching or ML training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-adduct-repair))
- **mass-spectral-analog-search**: Use when you have MS/MS reference spectra (MGF from MZmine, or a USI list in CSV/TSV) and want to find matching or analog spectra in thousands of indexed public datasets (GNPS/MassIVE, Metabolomics Workbench, MetaboLights, NORMAN). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-analog-search))
- **mass-spectral-baseline-estimation**: Use when raw MALDI-MS or ESI-MS intensity traces show an irregular, drifting baseline that must be removed before peak detection or normalization, and m/z/intensity data are held as plain data frames. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-baseline-estimation))
- **mass-spectral-binning-0-1-da**: Use when comparing predicted tandem mass spectra (from ICEBERG or baseline simulators) against ground-truth library spectra and you need a dataset-wide average cosine similarity on a common binned representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-binning-0-1-da))
- **mass-spectral-bitterness-classification**: Use when the input is an unlabeled EI-MS or ESI-MS/MS positive-mode mass spectrum (structure unknown or 'dark' metabolome) and the goal is a bitter/non-bitter class prediction directly from spectral features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-bitterness-classification))
- **mass-spectral-clustering-visualization**: Use when you have computed MS2DeepScore embeddings for annotated MS/MS spectra (e.g. a 3601-spectrum test set with InChIKeys) and need to verify or display that the embeddings group spectra by chemical superclass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-clustering-visualization))
- **mass-spectral-component-extraction**: Use when raw GC-MS data (netCDF or mzML format) contains overlapping chromatographic peaks with co-eluting ions that cannot be resolved by retention time alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-component-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-data-augmentation**: Use when when you have limited real GC-MS overlapped peak data but need thousands of labeled examples to train a deep learning model for mass spectral deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-data-augmentation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-data-formatting**: Use when when you have raw mass spectral data in .mgf, .msp, .mzML, or .lbm2 file formats and need to search against a spectral library using entropy similarity or Flash Entropy Search. Also apply this skill before building spectral library indices or computing entropy-based compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-data-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-data-post-processing**: Use when you have unlabeled MS1/MS2 features from herbal or TCM plasma/tissue samples and need to bridge in vivo metabolites back to prototype components without predicting structures beforehand. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-data-post-processing))
- **mass-spectral-data-validation**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS spectral data in vendor formats or unvalidated .mgf files before feeding them into the specXplore importing pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-data-validation) · 5 copies)
- **mass-spectral-database-retrieval**: Use when you have a curated compound list with labels or properties of interest and need the corresponding experimental mass spectra (EI-MS or ESI-MS/MS, positive ion mode) to run a spectrum-based prediction model, and the compounds' spectra are known to be deposited in MassBank or DrugBank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-database-retrieval))
- **mass-spectral-database-search**: Use when tandem MS/MS spectra must be annotated by searching against a large molecular structure database (e.g., DNP, PubChem, MoNA) and candidate structures need probabilistic scoring plus FDR-estimated identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-database-search--aafeae9cb7057f3e))
- **mass-spectral-database-search**: Use when experimental tandem MS spectra need to be matched to known molecules or previously unreported molecule variants from databases such as PubChem, COCONUT, or NPAtlas, especially at atlas scale (e.g. 483M GNPS spectra vs 87M molecules). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-database-search--5eee16e1bd231c0a))
- **mass-spectral-duplicate-counting**: Use when integrating multiple OMSLs (e.g., GNPS .MGF, MoNA .MSP, MassBank, MS-Dial-VS17) and you need to measure cross-library redundancy: the fraction of unique chemicals shared between two or more libraries and per-library duplicate spectra counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-duplicate-counting))
- **mass-spectral-embedding-computation**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectra (mgf, msp, mzml, mzxml, json, usi) in positive and/or negative ionization mode and need a numeric representation for cross-mode similarity scoring or chemical-space visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-embedding-computation))
- **mass-spectral-embedding-quality-estimation**: Use when computing MS2DeepScore embeddings or cross-ionization-mode Tanimoto similarity predictions and you need to flag spectra with low information content or dissimilarity to training data before trusting the predicted similarities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-embedding-quality-estimation))
- **mass-spectral-envelope-visualization**: Use when simulating theoretical precursor isotopic envelopes of a defined peptide sequence at user-defined 13C enrichment levels (e.g., natural 1.07% vs 50%) for SIP proteomics validation and figure generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-envelope-visualization))
- **mass-spectral-feature-alignment**: Use when when you have separate LC-MS peak tables for unlabeled (C12) and labeled (C13) isotope tracer experiments and need to identify which features correspond to the same metabolite across the two labeling conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-feature-annotation**: Use when you have m/z values from spatially-resolved mass spectrometry imaging (e.g., MALDI-MSI, DESI-MSI) and need to assign molecular formulae to thousands of features with higher precision than traditional LC-MS approaches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-annotation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-feature-connectivity-analysis**: Use when investigating which LC-MS/MS features are adjacent to one selected feature in a pairwise spectral similarity matrix, and you need to expand that neighborhood interactively by adjusting similarity thresholds and connectivity limits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-connectivity-analysis))
- **mass-spectral-feature-grouping**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics MS/MS spectra from multiple features and need to identify which features belong to the same molecular family or are related by biotransformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-grouping) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-fingerprint-generation**: Use when you have unaligned MS2 spectra from one or more samples (in formats like .mgf, .mzML, or .mzXML) and need to compare them in a retention-time-agnostic manner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-fingerprint-generation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-intensity-binning**: Use when raw GC-EI-MS peak lists (m/z / relative-intensity pairs, e.g. NIST 20 msp records) must be converted into a discrete intensity vocabulary before training or running a spectra-to-SMILES transformer, or when choosing among binning schemes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-intensity-binning))
- **mass-spectral-library-annotation**: Use when untargeted LC-QTOF-MS/MS screening data (e.g. MS-DIAL-exported feature tables and .msp/.mgf spectra) must be annotated against MS/MS libraries plus plastic-related and GC–MS-derived structure databases to yield identified compounds with library vs unknown status. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-annotation--54a4eee1e88826b5))
- **mass-spectral-library-annotation**: Use when you have extracted MS/MS spectra (.msp) from an untargeted metabolomics study where library search leaves many spectra unannotated and you want fingerprint-based additional annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-annotation--922e7fcfab8918cd))
- **mass-spectral-library-benchmark-execution**: Use when you have access to the Flash Entropy Search implementation and want to validate that real-time spectral library querying achieves the reported timing and throughput metrics under the same library sizes, query parameters, and measurement methodology documented in the paper. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-library-benchmark-execution) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-library-cleaning**: Use when an annotated MS/MS spectral library (GNPS, MassBank, MoNA) contains inconsistent metadata — missing SMILES, salt-formatted SMILES, parent masses computed from molar mass, mismatched adducts or annotations — and a curated subset with validated structure annotations is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-cleaning))
- **mass-spectral-library-construction**: Use when constructing a plant-specific annotation reference from public MS2 spectra and MS1 metabolite tables to feed a tidyMass/metID-based annotation workflow, and per-source metabolite counts must be tracked and validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-construction))
- **mass-spectral-library-curation**: Use when building or updating an open spectral library from a plate-based high-throughput LC-MSn acquisition, where compound metadata must be reconciled with PubChem/LOTUS/DrugBank identifiers and spectra merged into InChIKey-indexed MSn trees. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-curation))
- **mass-spectral-library-data-handling**: Use when raw or public-library MS/MS spectra (mgf, msp, mzML, mzXML, JSON, USI) must be cleaned, peak-filtered, binned, and split by compound before training or benchmarking spectral similarity models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-data-handling))
- **mass-spectral-library-file-parsing**: Use when the input is a mass spectral library file in .msp format and downstream work (metadata annotation, identifier conversion, or validation of InChI/SMILES/CAS attributes) requires the per-record metadata fields to be loaded and exposed first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-file-parsing))
- **mass-spectral-library-generation**: Use when you have MS1-level LC/HRMS mzXML/mzML/netCDF data from an untargeted metabolomics study (possibly n&gt;500) and need to build a reusable spectral library for annotating peaks, especially when MS2 data or MS2 library coverage is poor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-generation))
- **mass-spectral-library-import**: Use when deconvolved and aligned GC–MS compounds in a MetaboSet object need putative metabolite identities via spectral library matching, and the reference library must be loaded or swapped before running identifyComp. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-import))
- **mass-spectral-library-import-and-preprocessing**: Reference for ingesting MS/MS library files (msp, mzML, mzXML, MGF, JSON, metabolomics-USI) into matchms and running default-filter preprocessing with automatic YAML/report logging before library searching or ML training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-import-and-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectral-library-matching**: Use when a structurally characterized Mass2Motif (or other fragment/loss set) needs putative annotation or validation against curated MS/MS spectral libraries before transferring annotations to new datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-matching--d96cdbebfa644977))
- **mass-spectral-library-matching**: Use when the goal is to identify an unknown small molecule from its EI mass spectrum by ranking candidates in a spectral reference library (e.g., NIST 2017) and reporting recall@10. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-matching--3cc2b720bb7636b3))
- **mass-spectral-library-matching-cleanup**: Use when raw GNPS FBMN spectral match annotation tables (from MZmine2/MZmine3 feature tables of LC-HRMS/MS runs) need cleaning against defined match-quality thresholds before computing annotation rates or feeding downstream prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-matching-cleanup))
- **mass-spectral-library-msp-io**: Use when input is a mass spectral library file in .msp format whose records need loading into a Python tool, enrichment with metadata (SMILES, InChI, CAS), or export back to .msp after annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-msp-io))
- **mass-spectral-library-preprocessing**: Use when raw spectral library entries (e.g., GNPS JSON/MGF libraries) lack structural annotations like InChIKeys, or have inconsistent metadata (ionmode, charge, compound name, formula, parent mass), and structure-based analyses are planned. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectral-library-retrieval**: Use when querying mass spectra against a large spectral library (e.g. MoNA-hosted MassBank, GNPS, HMDB, ReSpect, FiehnLib entries) and you need to shrink the candidate set before running a full NIST cosine similarity search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-retrieval))
- **mass-spectral-library-search-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the query-time scaling of spectral library search modules (e.g., linear-time hash-table/bisection methods vs entropy-similarity search) across MassBank libraries of growing size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-search-benchmarking--70541bcae888e823))
- **mass-spectral-library-search-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the runtime scalability of MS2 spectral library search methods (e.g., ML-based analogue search vs score-based search with precursor preselection) on a fixed query set and library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-library-search-benchmarking--a81c0c7885c436b1))
- **mass-spectral-m-z-alignment**: Use when after you have (1) identified putative labelled features with intensity and m/z measurements from LC/MS data (e.g., via basepeak_finder output in geoRge), (2) defined a list of expected ionization adducts (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-m-z-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-main-peak-extraction**: Use when you need a coarse theoretical isotopic envelope (main peaks at integer neutron differences from the monoisotopic mass) for a metabolite with a known chemical formula or a peptide precursor, rather than the full isotopic fine structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-main-peak-extraction))
- **mass-spectral-matrix-prediction**: Use when you have raw GC-MS data with overlapped peaks in a specific retention time region and need to resolve the individual pure mass spectra of all components present in that region. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-matrix-prediction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-metadata-harmonization**: Use when you have multiple mass spectral libraries in different formats (NIST MSP + MOL folder, MoNA MSP, RIKEN MSP, SWGDRUG MSP) and need to merge them into a single, MS-DIAL-compatible MSP file with consistent SMILES assignments, Kovats retention indices (RI), and polarity annotations across all. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-metadata-harmonization) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-metadata-standardization**: Use when you have acquired EI or MS/MS spectral libraries from multiple public sources (NIST, RIKEN, MoNA, SWGDRUG, GNPS) with inconsistent metadata field layouts, missing or misplaced SMILES entries, undocumented retention indices, or mixed polarity modes, and you need to merge them into a single. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-metadata-standardization) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-missing-word-fraction-computation**: Use when when applying a pre-trained Spec2Vec Word2Vec model to new mass spectra (particularly those outside the model's training distribution), you need to assess whether peaks and neutral losses in query spectra have been seen during model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-missing-word-fraction-computation) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectral-molecular-network-annotation-merging**: Use when a GNPS molecular network already has mapped Mass2Motifs and a chemical class annotation dataframe is available, and the two annotation layers must be combined into one Cytoscape-ready network artifact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-molecular-network-annotation-merging))
- **mass-spectral-molecular-network-motif-mapping**: Use when you have GNPS molecular network outputs (graphml, clustered MGF, edges/nodes files) plus an MS2LDA experiment and need to link substructure motifs to network nodes/edges for annotation and visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-molecular-network-motif-mapping))
- **mass-spectral-network-annotation**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based) and MS2LDA LDA experiment output (Mass2Motif assignments with probability and overlap scores) from the same experiment, and you want to annotate network nodes with structural motifs and chemical classes to infer molecular. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-network-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-network-annotation**: Use when you have a GNPS-generated molecular network (graphml or JSON format) and corresponding MS2LDA experiment results or chemical class assignments, and you want to annotate network nodes with substructural motifs or chemical classes to facilitate structural interpretation and identify. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectral-network-annotation))
- **mass-spectral-peak-alignment**: Use when you have per-sample DIMS peak lists (e.g. SIM-stitched FT-ICR m/z peaks from MTBLS79 cow/sheep heart samples plus QC and blank) and need a cross-sample aligned matrix before replicate/blank/sample filtering and PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-alignment))
- **mass-spectral-peak-annotation**: Use when you have centroided MS2 spectra (in mzML format from data-dependent acquisition) and a list of known or suspect PFAS diagnostic fragment masses, and you need to systematically flag which detected features contain fragments characteristic of PFAS compounds (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-annotation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-peak-binning**: Use when filtered MS/MS peak lists must be turned into fixed-width numeric vectors (10,000 bins over 10–1000 m/z) to feed the MS2DeepScore base network or to build 200-dimensional spectrum embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-binning--edd02a6ac53ea2e4))
- **mass-spectral-peak-binning**: Use when raw mass spectral peak lists (m/z, intensity) must be converted into fixed-length numeric feature vectors for a classifier, or when spectra from different acquisition modes (EI-MS vs ESI-MS/MS) must be projected onto a common m/z scale for training or comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-binning--07f1464e79d04308))
- **mass-spectral-peak-embedding-with-formula-assignment**: Use when you have tandem mass spectra (e.g., MassSpecGym or NPLIB1/CANOPUS-format entries) with a known or inferrable chemical formula and need de novo candidate molecular structures ranked by sampling frequency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-embedding-with-formula-assignment))
- **mass-spectral-peak-overlap-detection**: Use when a matrix-annotation score (e.g. spectral S1 or morphological S2 in rMSIcleanup) falls below the user threshold and overlapped or isobaric peaks may share the same ion image as matrix-related ions, risking misclassification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-overlap-detection))
- **mass-spectral-peak-picking**: Use when raw LC-HRMS files (e.g., Thermo Orbitrap ID-X, MS1 scan range m/z 100–1000 at 60,000 resolution) must be converted into m/z and retention-time feature lists for differential comparison of preincubation and postincubation samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-picking))
- **mass-spectral-query-submission**: Use when you have one or more individual MS/MS spectra (in mzML, mzXML, or JSON format) and need to identify the compound(s) and their biological source by searching against a domain-specific spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-query-submission) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-ranking-metric-analysis**: Use when ranked candidate lists (e.g., the top-100 ranked compounds PS2MS yields per analyte) exist for the same test substances under two or more similarity metrics, and you must quantify which metric puts the true compound at rank 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-ranking-metric-analysis))
- **mass-spectral-relationship-matching**: Use when after peak detection and feature table generation when you have a collection of m/z, retention time, and intensity values and need to identify which features are related variants (isotopes, adducts, or fragments) of the same parent compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-relationship-matching) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-similarity-binning-and-stratification**: Use when you have predicted structural similarity scores (e.g., Tanimoto or Dice scores) for a large set of spectrum pairs and need to assess prediction accuracy across the full range of possible similarities. Critical when evaluating whether uncertainty filtering (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-binning-and-stratification) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-similarity-metric-selection**: Use when you have predicted spectra and ground-truth library spectra for the same molecules and need to score similarity directly (cosine similarity ratios) or via augmented-library matching with recall@10. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-metric-selection))
- **mass-spectral-similarity-network-construction**: Use when building metabolomic networks from GC-TOF-MS (or similar) feature spectra where many molecular features lack structural annotation and you want to connect unknowns to identified metabolites via spectral similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-network-construction))
- **mass-spectral-similarity-networking**: Use when metabolites in a measured feature table (e.g. GC-TOF-MS metabolites with BinBase identifiers) lack structural annotation or KEGG/PubChem mappings, and you need network edges connecting them via mass spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-networking))
- **mass-spectral-similarity-prediction**: Use when MS/MS spectra in MGF/h5py format must be scored against a spectral library (e.g., GNPS/MassBank) to retrieve structural analogues, or when similarity scores must reflect subtle structural modifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-prediction))
- **mass-spectral-similarity-scoring-across-samples**: Use when after XCMS feature detection, grouping, and retention time correction when you have aligned features with consistent retention times and intensity patterns across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-scoring-across-samples) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-similarity-search**: Use when you have a query .msp spectra file (e.g. unique CSA spectra variants exported for one study) and a reference .msp library (e.g. CSA or MS/MS library from another study), and you want to annotate compounds by spectral similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-search))
- **mass-spectral-similarity-searching**: Use when msp or .mgf spectra files (e.g., CSA composite spectra exported from MS1-only data, or DDA/DIA spectra) need compound-level annotations against a reference MS/MS library such as NIST 2020. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-searching))
- **mass-spectrometer-simulator-configuration**: Use when when you have a list of chemical compounds (with m/z values, retention times, and intensities) and need to simulate their acquisition behavior under a specific ionization polarity and mass spectrometer configuration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometer-simulator-configuration) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometrist**: Think and work like an expert Mass Spectrometrist. Use when a task calls for Mass Spectrometrist judgment. Reasons from ion formation, m/z resolution and mass accuracy, fragmentation, and calibrated ion statistics through ESI/APCI/MALDI tuning, CID/HCD/ETD MS/MS, isotope-pattern formula assignment, and spectral libraries (NIST, mzCloud, GNPS) under FDA/ICH M10/MSI tiers, while treating matrix suppression, PEG/siloxane/keratin contamination, decoy-driven FDR inflation, and unassigned adducts as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/mass-spectrometrist/skills/mass-spectrometrist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometrist**: Expert-thinking profile for Mass Spectrometrist (clinical / research): Reasons from ion formation, m/z resolution and mass accuracy, fragmentation, and calibrated ion statistics through ESI/APCI/MALDI tuning, CID/HCD/ETD MS/MS, isotope-pattern formula assignment, and spectral libraries (NIST, mzCloud, GNPS) under FDA/ICH M10/MSI tiers, while treating matrix suppression... ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/mass-spectrometrist) · ★13)

- **mass-spectrometry**: Ionization techniques (ESI, MALDI, EI), mass analyzers (Q-TOF, Orbitrap, Quadrupole), fragmentation, and tandem MS (MS/MS) ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/chemistry/mass-spectrometry))
- **mass-spectrometry-acquisition-metadata-handling**: Use when a study provides proprietary Thermo .raw files plus a sample-metadata CSV and centroided mzML inputs are required for downstream LC-MS preprocessing (e.g., Asari in pcpfm). - **mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectra with measured precursor m/z values but the ionization adduct type is unknown or ambiguous—particularly in metabolomics workflows where multiple adducts co-occur or in de novo formula annotation without access to curated spectral databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation))
- **mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation**: Use when an LC/MS feature table contains multiple features per analyte (isotope/adduct/fragment series) and you must assign quasi-molecular ion species, charge states, or neutral losses to reduce feature-list redundancy before statistical analysis or compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation))
- **mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation**: Use when you have tabulated pairwise mass differences from a MALDI-MS imaging dataset (via massdiff()) and need to identify which observed mass differences correspond to known chemical adducts (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M-H2O]+). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-adduct-assignment**: Use when when processing in-silico or experimental MS spectra records from databases with incomplete metadata, specifically when the adduct field is null or absent but the ionmode field (positive/negative polarity) is present. - **mass-spectrometry-adduct-identification**: Use when annotating MALDI-MSI peaks where suspected matrix or salt adducts (e.g., DHB) may inflate peak counts or cause false-positive metabolite identifications from exact mass alone. - **mass-spectrometry-adduct-nomenclature-and-formula-transformation**: Use when you have a neutral molecular formula (e.g., C3H8O2) and need to compute the adducted formula that will actually be observed in MS data; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-adduct-nomenclature-and-formula-transformation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-analysis**: Analyze proteomics, metabolomics, and spectral-library data with traceable preprocessing and identification control. ([anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science](https://github.com/anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science/tree/HEAD/OpenScience/runtime/skills/curated-scientific/mass-spectrometry-analysis) · ★1)
- **mass-spectrometry-annotation-engine-customization**: Use when when you have baseline MS/MS peak annotations from a known compound but need to refine them using newly available structural information (e. - **mass-spectrometry-annotation-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking annotation propagation on a molecular network: you need to quantify whether annotations reach nodes not directly connected to spectral library matches, not just first neighbours. - **mass-spectrometry-annotation-file-parsing**: Use when you have SIRIUS/ZODIAC annotation output files plus ground-truth formula lists (e.g. from MassIVE MSV000080502, MSV000081364) and need to reproduce or extend the molecular formula accuracy benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-annotation-file-parsing))
- **mass-spectrometry-annotation-rank-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating spectral annotation models on a candidate set and you need average rank / Rank@K metrics, or relative improvement percentages (e.g. 23.7% and 37.2%) against baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-annotation-rank-metric-computation))
- **mass-spectrometry-area-integration**: Use when processing full-scan AEMS spectra (precursor ion or neutral loss modes) from 384- or 1536-well plates where persistent high-intensity baseline signals obscure weaker, less well-defined analyte peaks that need quantification. - **mass-spectrometry-array-decompression**: Use when raw vendor MS files (e.g. SCIEX .wiff/.wiff.scan) contain compressed binary m/z and intensity arrays that must be decompressed and reassembled into usable spectra before peak integration or quantification. - **mass-spectrometry-base-peak-identification**: Use when after PuInc_seeker has identified putative incorporations in XCMS-processed LC/MS data, when you have paired unlabeled and labeled sample groups (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-base-peak-identification) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-batch-search**: Use when you have a set of MS/MS spectra (a .mgf file from MZmine or a list of USIs in .csv/.tsv) and need to find matching datasets and taxa in public metabolomics repositories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-batch-search))
- **mass-spectrometry-benchmark-analysis**: Use when you have implemented or modified a tandem mass spectrometry formula inference model and need to measure whether a specific architectural change (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-benchmark-analysis) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-benchmark-claim-extraction**: Use when a metabolomics/mass-spectrometry paper or abstract states numeric performance claims (runtimes, speedup factors, latency, accuracy, memory overhead) that must be tabulated and traced verbatim to source text. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-benchmark-claim-extraction))
- **mass-spectrometry-benchmark-design**: Use when when claiming that one mass spectrometry processing library achieves higher throughput than competitors, or when evaluating whether a new or optimized implementation delivers the expected computational efficiency gains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-benchmark-design) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-benchmark-metric-tabulation**: Decision support for quantifying how scaffold retrieval error limits spectra-conditioned molecular generation, when per-condition metric tables for both an oracle scaffold retriever (lookup table) and a predictive contrastive retriever are available alongside SPA values. - **mass-spectrometry-calibration**: Use when after peak detection when you have a detected peaks table with m/z values and need to correct systematic mass drift or inaccuracy before peak alignment across multiple batches. - **mass-spectrometry-centroided-spectra-visualization**: Use when the input is a Thermo Fisher Scientific raw Orbitrap file and you need to inspect or plot centroided MS/MS scans, a total ion chromatogram over MS1 scans, or extracted ion chromatograms directly from R. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-centroided-spectra-visualization))
- **mass-spectrometry-charge-state-handling**: Use when you have a list of monoisotopic [M+H]+ m/z peaks from positive-mode MALDI imaging and an LC-MS/MS peptide mass table (e.g., peptides.txt) and must correlate peaks to identified peptides by mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mass-spectrometry-charge-state-handling))
- **mass-spectrometry-chromatogram-extraction**: Use when you have raw profile LC-MS data in .mzML format and need to prepare candidate peak regions for classification by a neural network detector (e.g., QuanFormer). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-chromatogram-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-chromatogram-generation**: Use when after MS2 annotation and sample alignment have been completed in JPA, when you need to visualize ion chromatograms for quality control, validate feature identities, or export chromatographic evidence for specific metabolic features across multiple samples. - **mass-spectrometry-data-structure-interpretation**: Use when you have received an mzPeak archive (a ZIP file containing Parquet tables) and need to understand its internal structure, validate that spectrum metadata aligns with signal data, reconstruct m/z and intensity arrays (especially when null marking or zero-run stripping is present), or verify. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-data-structure-interpretation))
- **mass-spectrometry-data-structure-interpretation**: Use when you have converted multidimensional MS data (from Agilent .d, Bruker ion mobility .d, Thermo . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-structure-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-data-structure-mapping**: Use when after feature extraction and peak recognition have produced detected MS/MS spectra (precursor m/z, charge, retention time, and fragment ion peaks), and you need to export these spectra for external spectral database searching, cross-platform comparison, or archival in a format compatible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-structure-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-data-structure-validation**: Use when scripting against OpenMS from Python — after building/installing pyOpenMS and before writing mzML-loading or quantitation workflows, to confirm MSExperiment/FeatureMap objects work in Python. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-structure-validation))
- **mass-spectrometry-data-table-formatting**: Use when when you have raw or processed TWIM-MS data (arrival time and m/z values) from a mass spectrometry instrument and need to organize it into a feature table before biomolecular class assignment or CCS calculations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-table-formatting) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-data-validation**: Use when when reproducing or validating a tandem mass spectrometry denoising pipeline on mzML files with known feature precursor m/z and RT coordinates, compare pre- and post-filter counts of spectra and fragments at each major step (TIC cutoff, intra-spectrum grouping, frequency-based labeling). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-validation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-data-visualization**: Use when after applying retention time, abundance correlation, or EIC similarity-based feature grouping (e.g., via SimilarRtimeParam, AbundanceSimilarityParam, or EicSimilarityParam). Use when you need to visually confirm that grouped features belong to the same compound—i. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-visualization) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-data-visualization-pandas**: Use when when you have mass spectrometry data (mzML, Bruker .d, or CSV) loaded into a Pandas DataFrame with columns for m/z, retention time, ion mobility, or intensity values, and you need to render spectrum plots, chromatograms, mobilograms, or 2D peak maps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-visualization-pandas) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-data-visualization-with-pandas**: Use when your input is a Pandas DataFrame containing mass spectrometry measurements (m/z and intensity columns for spectra, retention time and intensity for chromatograms, or x, y, z for 2D/3D peak maps) and you need to generate static plots (matplotlib) or interactive web-based visualizations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-data-visualization-with-pandas) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-database-search**: Use when you have an unknown mass spectrum (or a representative metabolite spectrum from public data) and need to identify it by comparing it against a large reference library—particularly when the database contains billions of spectra and earlier tools like MASST are too slow or resource-intensive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-database-search) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-dataset-handling-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw or semi-processed MS/MS spectra in MSP format (e.g., from GNPS, Orbitrap instruments) and need to feed them into MSBERT or similar transformer-based embedding models for library matching, clustering, or similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-dataset-handling-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-dataset-post-processing**: Use when you have a formula-assigned FT-ICR MS dataset (CSV or tab-delimited table with molecular formulas and mass values) and need to quantify molecular chemodiversity, characterize elemental stoichiometry patterns, or explore transformation pathways across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-dataset-post-processing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-dataset-preprocessing**: Use when preparing MS/MS spectra (e.g., NPLIB1, NIST20, biomols, CASMI2022) for formula inference: candidate sets from mass decomposition are too large for exhaustive scoring and must be ranked/filtered before model training or evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-dataset-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-dataset-retrieval**: Use when a public LC-MS/MS dataset (e.g., a MassIVE accession such as MSV000078577) must be obtained from a repository to run dereplication, spectral library search, or continuous reanalysis against reference libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-dataset-retrieval))
- **mass-spectrometry-dda-scoring-reimplementation**: Use when reimplementing or evaluating DDA precursor-prioritization scoring: MS1 survey-scan ions with known prior fragmentation times (tf) need nonbinary dynamic-exclusion weights and a top-N MS2 target list per scan. - **mass-spectrometry-deconvolution-algorithm-application**: Use when you have raw DDA, DIA (MS^E, AIF, SWATH-MS), or MS1-only mass spectrometry data in mzML, mzXML, or netCDF format and need to deconvolute fragmentation spectra by linking precursor ions to their fragment ions based on retention time and m/z relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-deconvolution-algorithm-application) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-dereplication**: Use when you have a feature quantification table and an .MGF MS/MS summary from LC-MS/MS fractionation and must prioritize or annotate molecules before investing in purification. - **mass-spectrometry-detection-statistics-aggregation**: Use when raw Thermo .raw / .mzML flow injection acquisitions have been processed into per-file feature lists and you need to summarize how many unique compounds were detected exclusively in positive mode, exclusively in negative mode, or in both ionization modes. - **mass-spectrometry-dia-acquisition-simulation**: Use when the analysis goal is to simulate data-independent acquisition (e.g. all-ion-fragmentation or SWATH-style) over a chemical mixture, rather than Top-N DDA precursor selection, and the input is a set of generated or mzML-derived chemical objects. - **mass-spectrometry-domain-specific-language-comprehension**: Use when you need to translate user-facing mass spectrometry query intent (e.g., 'find all MS2 spectra with a precursor ion at m/z 572. - **mass-spectrometry-drift-time-processing**: Use when when you have raw ion mobility-mass spectrometry data (drift times, m/z values, and frame metadata) from DTIMS-MS, TWIMS-MS, or SLIM-based instruments and need to compute CCS values for structural characterization or database matching. - **mass-spectrometry-embedding-generation**: Use when when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral data (normalized peak intensities and m/z values) and need to convert individual spectra into fixed-dimensional vector representations for similarity-based metabolite matching or comparative analysis. - **mass-spectrometry-error-rate-estimation**: Use when quantifying how many spectra in a mass spectral library have missing or incorrect structure annotations, by re-deriving SMILES from compound names and comparing against original annotations. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-annotation**: Use when when you have peak-detected LC-MS/MS data (MGF files with MS1 and MS2 spectra, plus a feature abundance table from MZmine2) and need to assign chemical structures and molecular properties to individual MS1 features rather than relying on mass-to-charge alone. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-annotation-evaluation**: Use when an xsAnnotate/CAMERA peak list (e.g. faahKO or MM39) must be checked for correct isotope cluster membership and charge states, or when benchmarking a reimplementation of CAMERA's findIsotopes logic. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-based-molecular-networking**: Use when a reanalysis of public metabolomics data (e.g. GNPS-MassIVE MSV000081832) needs molecular families grouped by MS/MS fragmentation similarity and tested for differential activity between phenotypes such as healthy vs caries samples. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-deconvolution**: Use when you have a peak table from LC-MS peak picking software (e. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-deduplication**: Use when immediately after MZmine feature detection when you have both MGF (MS/MS spectra) and CSV (metadata) output files for one or both ionization modes and wish to construct a deduplicated molecular network. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-detection-validation**: Use when when you have processed LC-HRMS mzML files through a non-targeted preprocessing tool (e.g., XCMS, MZmine 2, MS-DIAL) and need to assess whether peak detection rates, isotopologue ratio fidelity, and alignment accuracy meet acceptable thresholds before downstream analysis. - **mass-spectrometry-feature-representation**: Use when after LC-MS feature clustering based on MS-DIAL peak character estimation, when you have grouped features that share similar chromatographic or spectral properties and need to select a single representative feature per cluster to reduce false positives and redundant annotations before.
