- **mass-spectrometry-file-format-handling**: Use when when you have Thermo Fisher Scientific Orbitrap .raw files (e.g., from Q Exactive HF instruments) and need to extract spectral, chromatographic, or metadata directly into R for downstream statistical analysis, benchmarking, or integration with Bioconductor workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-format-handling) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-file-format-parsing**: Use when you have raw MS/MS spectral data in one or more standard mass spectrometry file formats (.mgf, .msp, or .mzML) and need to convert them into a standardized bag-of-fragments representation for unsupervised topic modeling or substructure discovery workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-format-parsing) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-file-ingest-pipeline**: Use when new instrument acquisition files (mzML, mzXML, mzData) must flow continuously from an instrument PC into a QC-metrics database and dashboard with no manual file handling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-ingest-pipeline))
- **mass-spectrometry-file-inventory-management**: Use when you have uploaded MS/MS data to MassIVE with validated sample-information metadata and need to subset public files by sample attributes (organism, tissue type, extraction method, ionization source, pre-MS separation) to create reproducible, manageable cohorts for molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-inventory-management) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-file-level-feature-summarization**: Use when a collection of public LC-MS/MS files (e.g. those indexed in ReDU / MassIVE MSV000084206) needs an unsupervised, file-level overview of chemical content, or when PCA score plots colored by sample information are needed to explore repository-scale chemical annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-level-feature-summarization))
- **mass-spectrometry-file-standardization**: Use when you have raw or semi-processed mass-spectrometry peak data from XCMS, MSnbase, or other peak-picking tools in non-standard formats (MetaboAnalyst-like, Metabolights, vendor-specific), and you need to load them into MetaboShiny for compound identification, normalization, and statistical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-file-validation**: Use when after MSConvert has converted vendor-specific raw mass spectrometry data (ThermoFisher, Agilent, or equivalent formats) on a Linux system and before initiating analysis in MSThunder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-file-validation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-format-comparative-analysis**: Use when evaluating or comparing MS data formats (mzML, mzMLb, imzML, vendor RAW, mzPeak) across trade-off dimensions such as file size, data access speed, metadata support, interoperability, and long-term archival durability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-format-comparative-analysis))
- **mass-spectrometry-format-conversion**: Use when the input is an LC-MS data file in .mzML or Thermo .raw format and the goal is to parse it into flat per-spectrum records (spectrum_id, rt, mz, intensity) for downstream viewing, tabulation, or reuse without a GUI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-format-conversion))
- **mass-spectrometry-format-conversion**: Use when when you have mass spectrometry spectra in one of the six supported formats (mzML, mzXML, msp, metabolomics-USI, MGF, JSON) and need to convert them to a different format for compatibility with downstream tools, or when importing raw spectra into matchms for the first time and need to. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-format-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-format-parsing**: Use when you have mzML or mzXML mass spectrometry data files and need to extract and validate spectral records (m/z and intensity arrays) for lossless compression, lossy transformation, or format conversion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-format-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-formula-prediction**: Use when given a positive-mode MS2 spectrum (or batch of converted vendor raw files) with an observed precursor m/z and you need a ranked list of candidate precursor molecular formulas scored by candidate score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-formula-prediction))
- **mass-spectrometry-formula-representation**: Use when a neural model over formula candidates (e.g., the MIST-CF Formula Transformer or FastFilter) needs a fixed-dimensional, permutation-ordered numeric encoding of integer element counts rather than learned embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-formula-representation))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragment-annotation**: Use when tandem MS/MS spectra from a low-resolution miniaturized mass spectrometer contain unannotated fragment peaks (e.g. nominal m/z 262 and 249) whose molecular formulas or structures must be predicted and cross-validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragment-annotation))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragment-matching**: Use when you have experimental fragment m/z peaklists from Q-Exactive orbitrap, Agilent Q-TOF, Bruker Q-TOF, or SCIEX Q-TOF UHPLC-HRMS/MS instruments (in CSV or mzML-derived table formats) and need to assign lipid identities using untargeted or targeted tandem MS data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragment-matching) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-algorithm-implementation**: Use when you must enumerate all single- and double-bond-cleavage fragments of small molecules (0–2000 Da) from a structure database so tandem mass spectra can be matched against millions of candidate structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-algorithm-implementation))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-graph-serialization**: Use when building theoretical fragment sets from chemical structure databases (e.g., PubChem-derived SDF/MGF inputs) for DEREPLICATOR+ spectral annotation of LC-MS/MS natural product data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-graph-serialization))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-modeling**: Use when you are generating synthetic LC-MS/MS data for method validation, algorithm benchmarking, or co-fragmentation analysis, and need to model how specific biomolecules (peptides, nucleosides, or other chemical formulas) fragment under collision-induced dissociation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-modeling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-motif-visualization**: Use when a trained topic model over MS/MS fragmentation data (Mass2Motifs assigned to spectra) needs interactive visual exploration before motif characterization, e.g. filtering motifs by degree prior to biochemical interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-motif-visualization))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-discovery**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral data (in positive or negative ion mode) and seek to discover recurring fragmentation and neutral-loss patterns that characterize molecular substructures across a dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-discovery) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-modeling**: Use when when building a re-usable spectral reference library for lipidomics workflows where you need to match experimental MS/MS spectra against a comprehensive theoretical fragmentation model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-modeling) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-scoring**: Use when evaluating small-molecule identification accuracy of a mass spectral database-search method on spectra with known true structures, such as benchmarking top-1/3/5/10 accuracy on the PNNL lipid library against LMSD compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-scoring))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-spectrum-processing**: Use when you have paired genome + LC-MS/MS samples and need to turn raw fragmentation spectra (GNPS job output or cryptic spectra) into per-sample MS/MS fingerprints for BGC linking or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-spectrum-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-identification**: Identify and quantify support for analytes from mass spectrometry with explicit acquisition context, calibration, false-discovery control, and isomer-aware evidence. Use for GC-MS, LC-MS, MS/MS, accurate mass, isotope patterns, adducts, fragments, library searching, proteomics, metabolomics, and unknown identification. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/mass-spectrometry-identification) · ★4)
- **mass-spectrometry-image-linescan-projection**: Use when you have 2D DESI-MSI hyperspectral images of a tissue with an approximate linear developmental gradient (e.g., maize root tip) and need to assign mass signatures to developmental enrichment clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-linescan-projection))
- **mass-spectrometry-image-normalization**: Use when after loading a pixel array (NumPy format) and its associated metadata JSON file from MSIGen, when you need to account for pixel-to-pixel variations in total ion signal or when comparing relative abundances of multiple ions within or across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-normalization) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing**: Use when when preparing raw mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) ion images for deep learning–based representation learning, especially when you need to generate augmented image pairs that preserve domain-specific artifacts (photon counting noise, missing pixels) while varying appearance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw or preprocessed single-channel (2D array) or multi-channel (spectral) ion images from mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) data and need to train a contrastive deep learning model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-image-reconstruction**: Use when you have paired .imzML (XML metadata) and . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-reconstruction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-image-segmentation**: Use when a peak-picked MSI dataset (e.g., imzML loaded into Cardinal as a processed MSImagingExperiment) must be partitioned into chemically homogeneous tissue segments without class labels, and the expected number of segments is known from a reference figure or must be selected by scanning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-segmentation))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-classification**: Use when raw MALDI MSI spectra (e.g. imzML/HDF5 with tens of thousands of m/z variables) must be classified into tissue classes (normal vs tumor) with per-pixel probabilities, and the probabilistic outputs must be mapped back into image space to localize classification uncertainty such as at tumor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-classification))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-clustering**: Use when imzML MSI pixel spectra need unsupervised segmentation into tissue clusters, especially when t-SNE embedding parameters (perplexity, learning rate, iterations) must be tuned and compared via Silhouette score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-clustering))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-import**: Use when you have raw MS imaging data in imzML (continuous or processed) or Analyze 7.5 format and need to load it into R for spectral processing, normalization, peak-picking, or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-import) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-interpretation**: Use when when you have preprocessed MALDI-MSI data (in msimat format) and want to determine whether abundant peaks are actually molecular adducts of simpler parent ions rather than distinct metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-interpretation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-loading**: Use when you have raw or preprocessed MS imaging data archived as an RDS file or from a Zenodo deposit that includes the full m/z feature set (e.g., 10,200 m/z values spanning 150–1000 m/z range) and spectrum count (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-loading) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing**: Use when you have raw or processed MSI data (in imzML or rMSIproc formats) and need to identify and annotate matrix-related peaks before statistical analysis or metabolite identification. Use it specifically when your MSI experiment employed a chemical matrix (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing**: Use when you have DESI-MSI imzML/NetCDF (cdf) pixel spectra from elongating root sections arranged along a linear developmental gradient and need to identify metabolite mass signatures and group them by developmental enrichment pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-representation**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry imaging data (2D or 3D spatial coordinates with full mass-to-charge spectra) and want to train a probabilistic deep learning classifier for tumor delineation or tissue classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-representation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when pixel-wise peak features from one or multiple MALDI MSI samples must be jointly projected into a low-dimensional embedding (UMAP) and density-clustered (HDBSCAN) for segmentation or sample-outlier flagging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-dimensionality-reduction))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-line-scan-data-handling**: Use when you have raw line-scan MSI data from a vendor instrument (Agilent, Bruker, Thermo, or open-source .mzML format) and need to extract ion images for specified m/z targets with spatial binning and tolerance-based filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-line-scan-data-handling) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-peak-table-processing**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table matrix (pixels x m/z ion peaks with X/Y coordinates) contains background pixels and randomly distributed noise ions that must be detected and filtered before spatial pattern recognition or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-peak-table-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-preprocessing**: Use when raw DESI-MSI root-imaging data (NetCDF .cdf or MATLAB workspace .mat files) must be converted into per-sample mass signature lists before gradient clustering or cross-replicate comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-signal-preprocessing**: Use when raw MALDI-MSI imzML pixel spectra must be made comparable across a tissue section (TIC normalization), denoised, and background-corrected before clustering or segmentation with MSIght. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-signal-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-spectra-embedding**: Use when a trained massNet-style VAE over high-dimensional MALDI MSI spectra (e.g., 85,062 m/z variables) exists and you need to inspect its 5-D latent space or check normal-vs-tumor separability in a 2D UMAP map. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-spectra-embedding))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-vendor-format-support**: Use when your input mass spectrometry imaging data is in a proprietary vendor format (Bruker .d/.baf, or other binary formats not natively supported by MSIGen) and you need to convert it to an open, readable format (mzML or processed binary) compatible with MSIGen's msigen() function. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-vendor-format-support) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-instrument-format-compatibility**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry data from an instrument not yet validated in your pipeline (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-instrument-format-compatibility) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-instrument-resolution-modeling**: Use when when correcting LC-MS fractional abundances of measured isotopologues (FAM) from isotope labeling experiments where instrument resolution (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-instrument-resolution-modeling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-intensity-drift-correction**: Use when mS quantification data exhibits intensity drift—a systematic decline or variation in detector response across the run sequence. Intensity drift is particularly common in long measurement sessions and compromises the accuracy of feature-by-sample intensity matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-intensity-drift-correction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-intensity-encoding**: Use when converting vendor MRM/MS raw files to a compact, computation-oriented binary format and file size or fast web-side rendering of hundreds of chromatograms is the goal. - **mass-spectrometry-intensity-filtering**: Use when MS/MS spectra contain many low-intensity noise peaks — e.g. prior to peak annotation, library comparison, or spectrum visualization — and you need to restrict peaks to a fraction of the base peak intensity and cap their number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-intensity-filtering))
- **mass-spectrometry-ion-formula-assignment**: Use when when you have a metabolite structure (SMILES or molecular graph) and need to predict its ionization behavior in a mass spectrometry experiment using a specific derivatizing matrix or ionization mode. Use this skill when the expected ions are non-standard (i. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-ion-formula-assignment) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-ionization-mechanism-interpretation**: Decision support for choosing an ionization source (EI 70 eV, EI 15 eV, CI-methane, CI-isobutane) when designing or interpreting GC-MS isotopologue detection for 13C/15N labeling experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-ionization-mechanism-interpretation))
- **mass-spectrometry-ionization-mode-handling**: Use when when you have a feature table from LC-MS preprocessed data (e.g. from asari v1.9.2) and need to annotate ions and infer neutral mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-ionization-mode-handling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-library-curation**: Use when when preprocessing a public MS/MS spectral library (e.g., GNPS) for machine learning and you discover discrepancies between expected and observed compound counts after filtering by instrument type, or when a known instrument metadata issue (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-curation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-library-downloading**: Use when you need to quantify the adduct/ion-species composition of public LC-MS2 spectral libraries (MoNA, GNPS) or need those raw library entries as inputs for an ion-species bias analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-downloading))
- **mass-spectrometry-library-indexing**: Use when you have a large MS/MS experiment (mzML format) requiring lipid annotation and need to match experimental spectra against &gt;10 million theoretical lipid fragments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-indexing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-library-matching**: Use when you have a set of unannotated MS/MS consensus spectra (e.g., an .mgf from MZmine or GNPS molecular networking) and need to identify which ones match metabolites from a specific taxonomic domain such as microbes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-matching--f8a5aa74ab04f373))
- **mass-spectrometry-library-matching**: Use when when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum (with ≥10 peaks, precursor m/z, and at least 5 fragment ions) and need to identify it by comparing against a curated spectral library with annotated InChIKeys or chemical structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-matching) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-library-matching**: Use when untargeted metabolomics MS/MS peak annotations yield too many candidate structures per formula (isomers), and predicted retention times from an HILIC or RPLC Retip model can be used to exclude unlikely candidates in MS-FINDER compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-matching--312e3555fe9fcc87))
- **mass-spectrometry-library-ranking**: Use when you have a set of unidentified tandem mass spectra (queries) and need to identify them by matching against a curated reference library (e.g., GNPS Orbitrap dataset). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-ranking) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-library-search-retrieval**: Use when when you have an unknown MSMS spectrum (precursor m/z and fragment ions) and need to discover structurally related compounds from a spectral library. - **mass-spectrometry-mass-peak-matching**: Use when you have a target molecular weight (1–5 decimal digits) from a high-resolution mass spectrum of an unknown natural-product mixture and need to rank candidate structures from seed scaffolds (.cIdx files). - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-caching**: Use when you plan to perform repeated queries or filtering on MS metadata attributes (e.g., extract all MS2 spectra with collision energy &gt; 30 eV, or collect all scans in a retention time window) across a large MZA HDF5 file. - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-extraction**: Use when Thermo RAW files (e.g. downloaded from PRIDE accessions) need instrument-, run-, and scan-level metadata extracted into structured TSV/CSV tables for QC, repository submission, or batch annotation workflows.
